Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, question 3, "Was your qualifying relative married? Yes. See Married person"; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement, "two or more persons, each of whom would be able to claim the person as a dependent but for the support test, together provide more than half of the person's support" (the support test of a qualifying relative; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"). The reply now requires dependent.joint_return of every entry and refuses dependent.multiple_support for every entry, as the deepseek family's earlier ruling on this line decided for the same deviations (joint_return is asked by the project only on the qualifying-child path; for a qualifying relative the married answer, which includes being on a joint return, decides): 173 completed unknowns on joint_return (for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a, project yes) and 120 on multiple_support (for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_c, a qualifying child); generated 126 dep_b, a qualifying child with multiple_support yes, is refused. The 9 completed values (project yes, candidate no; for example classm.f8889.1.deepseek dep_b, generated 20, 235, 436, 464) are all entries with married No and joint_return Yes, a record the interview cannot produce: dependent.married asks whether a joint return is filed that includes this person (as the deepseek family's earlier ruling). As this family's earlier ruling, it also asks dependent.lawful of household_member entries (that relationship choice already states "if your relationship did not violate local law"; the project asks lawful only for other and sibling_descendant) (14 completed, for example classm.clcw26.2.deepseek dep_b). Its dependent.kidnapped question and kidnap and tiebreaker gates are gone; their differences are the project's cited kidnap_residency and qc_tiebreak refusals on refused returns. No completed-return amount differs. The reworded input questions play no part: the deviation reads inputs whose questions did not change, or reads the changed ones as before. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 6,144 of 7,537 agree; 890 candidate-unknown, 316 project-refuses, 110 project-unknown, 72 value, 5 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 315 candidate-unknown, 9 value, 1 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.json`). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 14, Test 2; p501 p. 9, Temporary absences and Death or birth. As the earlier ruling (no part-year route; the 78 completed project-unknowns on dependent.absence_no_return, for example classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, are on returns that do not file head of household, where the interview never needs the line). It now inherits the reply's dependent.claimed instead of a kidnapped question: 123 completed unknowns on joint_return (for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a), the multiple-support refusal (generated 126 dep_b), and 5 values (project true, candidate false) on the married No and joint_return Yes records the interview cannot produce (classm.f8889.1.deepseek dep_b, stage21.f1.lower_bound_still_decides dep_b, generated 235 and 436). No completed-return amount differs. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 6,335 of 7,537 agree; 518 project-unknown, 329 candidate-unknown, 328 project-refuses, 22 value, 5 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 127 candidate-unknown, 82 project-unknown, 5 value, 1 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.json`). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i2441 p. 3, Line 2, "Complete columns (a) through (d) for each qualifying person". As the earlier ruling: the project counts one per entry and f2441.qp_count filters by dependent.dc_qp; the reply's count is 1 or 0 by its own dc_qp, so the 2,046 completed values (project 1, candidate 0) are entries the project's filter does not count, and it inherits the reply's dc_qp unknowns and refusal. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 1,496 of 7,537 agree; 4,349 value, 1,619 candidate-unknown, 73 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 2,046 value, 308 candidate-unknown, 1 candidate-refuses. Ruling: model error; i8962 p. 8, Line 2b, dependents "who are required to file"; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement, "two or more persons, each of whom would be able to claim the person as a dependent but for the support test, together provide more than half of the person's support" (the support test of a qualifying relative; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"). As the earlier ruling, the reply's formula represents the project's choose on ptc_files, and the 28 completed project-unknowns (for example classm.clcw.2.deepseek dep_a) are returns with no Form 8962, where the interview does not ask ptc_files and no printed amount reads the line. The class changes from scope: the line now inherits the reply's multiple-support refusal on dependent.claimed, and generated 126 dep_b (a qualifying child with multiple_support yes, project $0) is refused on a completed return. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 7,014 of 7,537 agree; 449 project-unknown, 73 candidate-refuses, 1 value; on returns the full interview completes: 28 project-unknown, 1 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.json`). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; f8863 p. 2, line 31; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit; p970 p. 11. As the earlier ruling: the reply asks dependent.edu of every entry (856 completed unknowns where the project hides the line, for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a) and requires dependent.edu_tin_issued (10 completed, for example classm.f8863.2.deepseek dep_a), against the p970 p. 11 rule for a timely ITIN or ATIN application. It also inherits the reply's joint_return question on dependent.claimed (2 completed, classm.f8863.4.deepseek and classm.f8863.4 dep_c, project $9,553). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 4,586 of 7,537 agree; 2,864 candidate-unknown, 68 project-refuses, 12 project-unknown, 7 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 868 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.json`). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One person listed in the Dependents section"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 and younger than you (or your spouse), or under age 24, a full-time student and younger than you (or your spouse), or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1: the person meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "Open: the qualifying-child tiebreaker when the child is also another person's qualifying child (other_claimant), and the kidnapped-child residency exception, whose conditions no supplied input answers. Chosen: you are treated as able to claim the child as your qualifying child, and residency is the months answer (more than half the year, at least 7)."

  - id: dependent.h_qr_rel
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: related to you (or, on a joint return, to your spouse) in a way that need not live with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_member
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: a listed relative, or any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_rel, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_not_qc_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Who wasn't a qualifying child of any taxpayer for 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: the person meets the conditions to be your qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_member, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_not_qc_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
    note: "The sheltered-workshop exception to the gross income test is not computed; the gross income answer is used as given."

  - id: dependent.h_citizen
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]

  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: you can claim the child tax credit for this person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 and 5: you can claim the credit for other dependents for this person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.h_odc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500 per other dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.h_actc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 5 (line 4 times $2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 7 (line 6 times $500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 16b (children times $1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 1: you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home that was the main home for all of 2025 of your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, except under a multiple support agreement"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 2: this person lived in your home for more than half of the year and is a qualifying person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_rel, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: true}]}
    note: "Excludes a dependent who is your dependent only because the person lived with you all year (relationship not one of the listed relatives). The divorced-parent and multiple-support exclusions are covered by the refusals on dependent.claimed."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An absence was counted as time lived in the home although it was not reasonable to assume the absent person would return, or the home was not kept up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Step 3: this person is your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule]
    note: "Open: the tiebreaker when the child is also another person's qualifying child. Chosen: you are treated as able to claim the child."

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child who has a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person for Form 2441: a qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent, or a dependent not able to care for themselves who lived with you for more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                    - op: test
                      all:
                        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}

  - id: dependent.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  - id: dependent.h_dc_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent if this person is a qualifying person, otherwise zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_one_cent}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons this entry adds to Form 2441, line 2 (1 or 0)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_cent, whole: {param: sc.hundred_cents}, cap: 100}
    note: "A choose cannot return a Count, so the 1-or-0 count is formed as the whole-number percentage of 1 cent (or 0) over 100 cents."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp
    note: "A person 14 or older at the end of 2025 was over age 12 all year; one who was 13 at the end of 2025 is over 12 at the time of care only if dc_over12_care is answered yes; one 12 or younger never is."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp
    note: "The instruction not to include in column (d) the benefits on line 28 is applied to the Form 2441 total (line 30), not per person, because no input allocates the benefits among persons."

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI counted on Form 8962, line 2b (only dependents required to file because their income meets the filing threshold)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet line 3: total adjustments (lines 2a, 2b and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Education credit may be figured for this claimed dependent (expenses paid, Form 1098-T requirement met, student TIN issued by the due date)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26 route this student to lines 27 through 30 (American opportunity credit), with the institution's EIN provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: a student who passes lines 23 to 26 but whose institution EIN is unavailable. Chosen: the American opportunity credit is not claimed (the EIN is required for it) and the student goes to line 31, for which the EIN is not required."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student goes to line 31 (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_over_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Assistance and refunds exceed one academic period's expenses (per-period allocation not computed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Lifetime learning credit: adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_llc
```
